Transport drops are deliberately excluded from real_failures ('the NETWORK failing, not the task') so it retries forever — correct when it IS a flaky node, but a fault that FOLLOWS THE TASK across all three nodes is not a node fault. app-js is the longest generation in the run. The detector cannot tell 'flaky link' from 'this generation is too long to survive a stream', and treats both as network.

My 'same error on 3 nodes means the task is the variable' inference was wrong — 3 nodes on one LAN share enough that a fault follows work without being caused by it. Cost: 45min of BUILD.

Fix = kill the process group. Detector added to tick.py.
